Prayer in W.P.23741/2018:
Writ Petition filed under Article 226 of the Constitution
of India to issue a Writ of Certiorarified Mandamus,Calling for
the records of the 2nd respondent relating to the impugned order
dated 08.09.2017 uploaded in the website of the 1st respondent
in so far as the Petitioners herein are concerned, quash the
same as illegal, arbitrary and devoid of merit and
consequentially direct the respondents herein to permit the 1st
petitioner (having DIN No. 00826458) and 2nd petitioner (having
DIN No. 00826416) to get reappointed as director in the Company
or appointed in any other Company without any hindrance.

C O M M O N O R D E R
In all these writ petitions, the respective writ petitioner
challenged the notification of the respondents dated 08.09.2017,
01.11.2017, 05.09.2017, 11.09.2017, 03.11.2019 and 04.09.2017
disqualifying the respective writ petitioner as Director of the
respective Company. Consequently, the petitioners seek for
permission to get themselves reappointed as Director of any
Company or appointed as Director in any Company without any
hindrance.
2. It is submitted by the learned counsel appearing for the
petitioners that the issues raised in these cases were already
raised by similar writ petitioners questioning the very same
notifications and that those issues were considered and decided
by this Court in favour of those petitioners in its earlier
order dated 03.08.2018 made in a batch of cases in
W.P.Nos.25455/2017 etc., wherein this Court, after hearing both
parties, allowed those writ petitions by setting aside the
impugned notifications therein.
3. Learned Additional Solicitor General appearing for the
respondents submitted that though the issues raised in the
present cases are also covered by the above said earlier order
passed by this Court in the batch of cases dated 03.08.2018,
Writ Appeals are filed against the said order and the same are
pending. However, he admitted to the position that no stay is
granted in the Writ Appeal staying the operation of the order
passed in W.P.No.25455/2017 etc. batch case dated 03.08.2018.
4. Therefore, it is an admitted fact that the subject matter
notifications under challenge in these writ petitions were
already challenged before this Court in the earlier batch by
raising the very same contentions/ grounds as raised in these
writ petitions and that those contentions/ grounds were
170
https://hcservices.ecourts.gov.in/hcservices/

considered by this Court in detail and a common order was passed
on 03.08.2018 in those writ petitions thereby allowing the same
and setting aside the very same impugned notifications. After
going through the above said common order passed in detail and
the reasoning and findings rendered therein, I am in full
agreement with the above decision made in respect of the very
same impugned notifications. Though it is stated that the said
common order is put to challenge by way of filing Writ Appeal,
it is seen that the said order has not been stayed by the
Division Bench. Hence, it is apparent that the order dated
03.08.2018 passed in similar matters is in force.
5.Considering the above stated facts and circumstances and in
view of the fact that the very same notifications
challenged in these writ petitions are the subject matter
in those cases and that this Court has allowed those writ
petitions by setting aside the impugned notifications
therein and further considering the fact that the said
order dated 03.08.2018 has not been stayed in Writ Appeal
and thus the same is in force even as on today and since
this Court is in full agreement with the findings rendered
therein, all these Writ Petitions are allowed and the
impugned orders are set aside by following the earlier
order dated 03.08.2018 passed in W.P.No.25455/2017 etc.,
batch. Consequently, the connected miscellaneous petitions
are closed. No costs.
 -s/d-
 Assistant Registrar(CS-IV)
